# Project Status

Sync batch: v180 · 2026-10-04 · re-verified after ADR-109 (public-alias deployment smoke, pinned runner image, zero lint/CI annotations), ADR-108 source re-verification and registry-derived test clocks; ADR-105 review-session dose and review-hour anchoring, the ADR-102 browser WebGPU ESM-resolution fix and ADR-101 Vercel output compatibility remain verified.
## فحص الإنتاج وتعليقات CI بلا أخطاء — 2026-10-04 (ADR-109، م46)

- **القياس (من مشغّل GitHub لا من هذه البيئة)**: `Deployment Smoke` كان يسقط بعد كل نشر Production لأن `DEPLOYMENT_SMOKE_PRODUCTION_URL` غير مضبوط والتوكن لا يملك ضبطه (403)، فيُفحص مضيف النشر الخاص المحمي (`blocked-by-auth`). الاسم المستعار العام من المشغّل: **7/7 مسارات `passed`، خروج 0** (run `37193849680`). والمسار الشهري يبقى أحمر حتى بعد تجديد التواريخ لأن صفحتي npmjs.com تردّان 403 للروبوتات. وآخر Quality Gate أخضر حمل 3 تحذيرات lint وتحذيري «Slow Test» وإشعار هجرة `ubuntu-latest`.
- **ما فُعل**: الاسم المستعار هو الافتراضي في نسختَي `deployment-smoke.yml` المتطابقتين (لا `environment_url` بعد الآن)؛ `ubuntu-24.04` في سطور `runs-on` التسعة (ثمانية ملفات) + حارس `workflow-runner-pin.test.ts`؛ `manual-on-403` لسجلّي npmjs.com كسجلّ Goethe؛ `actions/github-script@v8`؛ `reportSlowTests: null` (تقرير فقط). تحذيرات lint أُصلحت لا عُطّلت: `requireGuestIsolation` (جديدة) تفرض عزل الضيف وتُرمى عند الخرق، و`boundedFetch` في `sw.js` يحفظ سبب الفشل (`cause`) ووسم السياسة — بلا رفع جيل الحزمة (v180).
- **telc**: فُتح أرشيف `telc_deutsch_b2.zip` من المشغّل (HTTP 200، `Last-Modified` 2023-03-27) ورُبط بطبقة نص الـPDF: علامات طبعة 2019 الثلاث ✓ والحقائق السبع المثبّتة **7/7** (`pdftotext -raw` + توحيد المسافة قبل `%`)؛ فأُغلق قيد «رابط فقط» الذي علّقه الإقرار (الدليل: `docs/run-logs/2026-10-04-source-reverification/README.md`، آخر قسم).
- **البوابات (محليًا على الشجرة النهائية)**: `npm run check` خروج 0 — lint **0 أخطاء / 0 تحذيرات** (كانت 6 ثم 3) · `tsc` نظيف · `vitest` **1,257/1,257** في **181/181** (+3: حارس المشغّل ×2 وحارس عزل الضيف) · `secret:audit` · `source:audit --strict` 19/19 · `workflows:check` (8 ملفات) · `handoff:check` · `npm run build` 322/322؛ وخطوات CI التالية للـcheck (`secret:audit:history` · الحزمة كاملة عند `TZ=Asia/Tunis` 181/181 · حرس النطاقات Kiritimati/Niue/Berlin 7/7 لكلٍّ) ناجحة. المتصفح (e2e) لم يُعَد محليًا في هذا الصف: التغييرات لا تمسّ واجهة سوى مسار فشل التنزيل في `sw.js` وحارس الضيف، فيُقرأ من CI على هذا الرأس (فحوص PR #6).
- **حدود هذا الصف**: (1) `Deployment Smoke` بحدث `deployment_status` لم يُشغَّل بالتعبير الجديد (لا يُحاكى من PR)؛ المُثبَت أن السكربت ينجح على الاسم المستعار من المشغّل وأن التعبير يُقيَّم إليه؛ **أول دليل كامل هو نشر Production التالي بعد الدمج**. (2) الفحص على الاسم المستعار لا يربط الناتج بالـcommit المنشور (لا ترويسة بناء). (3) `release.yml`/`release-candidate.yml` ما زالا على `upload-artifact@v4` و`action-gh-release@v2` (مثبَّتان باختبارات، بلا run يُظهر تحذيرًا ولا صلاحية تشغيل تجريبي) — تُركا. (4) `manual-on-403` يحوّل فحص وصول السجلّين إلى مراجعة يدوية. (5) أرقام الحزمة المثبَّتة في README والتقارير المولَّدة (`public/offline-size-manifest.json`، `reports/*budget*`) هي قياس آخر بناء للمالك ولم تُعَد كتابتها من بيئة هذه الجلسة (zlib مختلف)؛ بناء هذه الجلسة أعطى JS **1,894,895** بايتًا مضغوطًا (+790 عن 1,894,105، من نصّ السجلّ والحارس الجديد) و`full` **5,767,731** (البصمة `cc849b0ddfb4`) — داخل الميزانية.
- **باقٍ على المالك**: دمج PR #6 (يُنشر تلقائيًا إلى Production)؛ مراجعة 2026-11-01 (`onnxruntime-web` ثم بقية السجلّات في 2026-11-03).

## بوابة المصدر وإعادة التحقق من 18 مصدرًا — 2026-10-04 (ADR-108، م45)

- **الحدث (مقاسًا)**: عند 2026-10-03T23:00Z خرج `source:audit --strict` بـ1 («stale 12») فسقط `check`، وسقط في `e2e` اختبار واحد (`critical-flows.spec.ts:81`) لأنه يؤكّد حالة تتبع تاريخ اليوم؛ ولم يتغيّر كود المنتج.
- **ما فُعل**: قُرئت المصادر الحيّة الـ18 (12 منتهية + 6 تنتهي في 2026-10-08/12) وقورنت بـ`observedState`/`claimAr`، وعُرض الجدول على المالك فأقرّ تسجيل 2026-10-04 للـ18 (الدليل في `docs/run-logs/2026-10-04-source-reverification/README.md`). صار `source:audit --strict` = **19/19 طازجة**؛ الاستحقاق التالي 2026-11-01 (`onnxruntime-web`) ثم 2026-11-03.
- **الأثر على المتعلم**: يعود الذكاء البعيد الاختياري (Gemini/OpenRouter) وتنزيل نماذج المتصفح، ويعرض مركز الامتحانات «ملف الصيغة موثّق وحديث».
- **فكّ ارتباط الاختبارات بالتواريخ**: `tests/helpers/source-verification-clock.ts` يشتق ساعة 10 ملفات وحدة و4 حالات e2e من السجلّ (محاكاة إعادة التحقق قبل التنفيذ كسّرت 11 ملفًا/44 حالة)؛ واختبار المركز يؤكّد تواريخ السجلّ لا حالةً تتبع اليوم، وثلاثة اختبارات جديدة تقيس «حديث / يقترب / يحتاج إعادة تحقق» بساعة مثبَّتة (الأخيرة لم تكن مغطّاة بأي اختبار واجهة)؛ وقاعدة النافذة تبقى مقيسة بتواريخ صريحة على كائن ثابت.
- **عطب أسبوعي كشفه أول CI لهذا الطلب (الأحد)**: اختباران في e2e (`P0 exam readiness…` و`progress and daily coach…`) يقرآن ساعة الآلة ويفتحان `/today`، والأحد يوم الراحة المخطَّط افتراضيًا (`isPlannedRestDay`) فلا مهمّة ولا `.mission-row`؛ نجحا مساء السبت وسقطا الأحد بلا تغيّر كود. ثُبِّتت ساعتهما على أربعاء (`page.clock.setFixedTime`) وأُعيد إنتاج الفشل محليًا قبل الإصلاح ونجاحهما بعده.
- **البوابات**: `tsc` نظيف · lint 0 أخطاء / 3 تحذيرات (كانت 6) · `vitest` **1,250/1,250** في **179/179** (+1 عقد المساعد) · `source:audit --strict` 19/19 · `workflows:check` و`handoff:check` ناجحان · `npm run build` ناجح · خطوات CI التالية للـcheck محليًا (`secret:audit:history`، الحزمة كاملة عند `TZ=Asia/Tunis`، حرس النطاقات) ناجحة · e2e محلي على Chromium 153 والساعة الحقيقية يوم الأحد: المكتبي **61/61** والجوال **61/61**. حالة CI لهذا الرأس في فحوص PR #6.
- **حدود هذا الصف**: الإقرار قرار المالك لا فتحٌ يدويّ مستقل لكل صفحة؛ أرشيف telc (طبعة 2019، ويعتمد عليه 225/75 و135/45) ثبت رابطه ولم يُفتح وقت الإقرار (ثم فُتح من مشغّل GitHub — ADR-109)؛ تحذيرا `public/sw.js` وتحذير `guest-session.ts` تُركت عمدًا في ذلك الصف (انظر ADR-108) ثم أُصلحت بعده في ADR-109 أدناه.
- **باقٍ على المالك**: مراجعة 2026-11-01. (فتح أرشيف telc وتثبيت `ubuntu-latest` كانا هنا ثم أُنجزا في ADR-109: الأول من مشغّل GitHub بـ7/7 حقائق، والثاني بتثبيت `ubuntu-24.04`.)

## كومة المراجعة وتقدير الامتحان — 2026-10-03 (ADR-107، م43)

- القياس قبل الشيفرة: `scripts/review-pile-simulation.ts` على المقرر الحقيقي — **2,189** بطاقة مستحقة بعد 96 درسًا، و**−0.83/يوم** بعدها (الكومة لا تتلاشى)، مقابل **68** في وتيرة «درس كل خمسة أيام».
- بوابة إفراج جُرِّبت نموذجًا أوليًا وقِيست ثم **رُفضت وحُذيت شيفرتها**: تقطع طابورًا يحميه اختبار، وتؤخّر الدرس الأخير 19 يوم دراسة، ولم توقف النمو (ADR-107 · «ما جُرِّب ورُفض»).
- ما نُفِّذ: `reviewBacklogPlan` (مصدر واحد لرقم الكومة وسقفها) + خطة تفريغ مرئية في `/review` + تصحيح `exam-target-forecast` الذي كان يحمّل الكومة كلها دقائق قبل الهدف (1,642 دقيقة لـ2,189 بطاقة)؛ صار المحسوب `min(due, perDay × daysRemaining)` والمؤجَّل حقلًا معلنًا `reviewsDeferredCount`.
- الاختبارات: **1,249/1,249** في **179/179** (+5 حالات `tests/unit/review-backlog-honesty.test.ts` تثبت تساوي `due` مع الطابور، وسقف التقدير، ونصّ «لا تُحتسب دينًا»)، بلا تخفيف عتبة ولا تعطيل ملف.
- **بوابة المصدر انقلبت حمراء بعد ذلك**: عند 2026-10-03T23:00Z تجاوزت 12 سجلات من 19 في `src/config/source-verification-registry.json` نافذة الثلاثين يومًا (يوم التقويم = `Africa/Tunis`)، فصار `npm run source:audit -- --strict` يخرج 1 («stale 12») ويوقف `check` و`e2e` — **لا بسبب كود هذا الجيل** (نفس الشجرة كانت خضراء قبل الانقلاب بعشرين دقيقة). لم نزِد `lastVerifiedAt` ولا خفّفنا السقوف: الوضع `manual-semantic-review` ويستلزم مراجعة بشرية للمصادر. أُصلح ما هو عيب اختبار فعلًا: 4 حالات وحدة و4 حالات e2e كانت تقرأ ساعة الآلة، صارت تثبّت ساعتها صراحةً، وبقيت قاعدة الصلاحية مقيسة في `source-freshness.test.ts`. الأثر على المتعلم الآن مقصود: أوضاع الذكاء البعيد محجوبة والمرشد المحلي يعمل.
- **CI لهذا الصف**: `30cfc87` أخضر (`check` 6د02ث ✓ · `e2e` 10د42ث ✓)؛ وأول دفعة منه `7cbb191` **سقطت** على `exam:format-claims:audit` داخل `prebuild` — بصمة مصادر التقرير تتحرّك بتعديل `exam-target-forecast.ts`؛ صُلح بتوليد التقرير ثم إعادة تثبيت البصمة بعد بناء حقيقي (`a37c5b0d6248` → `8f1f595b42f9`، full 5,766,321 → 5,767,057، JS 1,893,352 → 1,894,105) — والقاعدة: كتّاب التدقيقات بعد آخر تعديل شيفرة، ثم البناء، ثم التثبيت.
- **حدود هذا الصف**: لا مراجعة بشرية لصياغة سطر `/review` الجديد؛ وأرقام المحاكاة نموذج آلي لا قياس استبقاء؛ و«أيام التفريغ» تقدير خطّي.
- **باقٍ على المالك**: الوتيرة المعلنة أو مدة الجلسة أو عدد البطاقات المُقنَّنة لكل درس — قرار محتوى تربوي لا قرار أداة.

## معيار الدرس الواحد وإعادة تسمية «الاستقلال» — 2026-10-03 (ADR-106، م37)

- القياس على 96 درسًا × ثمانية أسئلة من البيانات لا من الانطباع: الهيكل سليم (0 بند مصحَّح بلا تبرير من أصل 1,733 بندًا؛ 24 بطاقة لكل درس وكلها داخل طابور المراجعة، صفر انحراف)، والفجوات كانت تأليفية لا برمجية: سُدّ منها 54 سطر «خطأ شائع» بشرح دون 20 حرفًا و53 سطر تريك دون 15 (المرحلة B) و64 درسًا بلا مهمة نقل مؤجلة (المرحلة C: 96/96 الآن)، وبقي 73 درسًا بلا رابط متطلب مؤلَّف. كل رقم مُغلق نُزِّل سقفه إلى 0.
- عقد `lesson-teaching-contract-v1` (`src/core/lesson/teaching-contract.ts`) + تقرير مُلزَم (`npm run lesson:contract:audit`) موصول بـ`prebuild` وبحلقة `handoff:check`: سبعة أسئلة صعبة لا تقبل الاستثناء وواحد مرقوم كفجوة؛ العتبات أسقف — التدهور يفشل والتحسّن يُلزَم بخفض السقف يدويًا.
- ما كان `independentKnowledgePassed` وعنوانه «جمعت محاولة مستقلة» صار `firstUnaidedRecallPassed` و«أول استدعاء بلا سند — تدريب»، وحالة الدليل المؤجَّل صارت «دليل الاستقلال: استرجاع مؤجل»، ونصّ الحدّ يذكر أن الاستقلال مهمة جديدة بعد ثلاثة أيام فأكثر. حساب `passed` و`basicTrainingPassed` والإتقان لم يُمَسّ: لا تجميد لأحد ولا ترقية لأحد.
- قياس هذه الجلسة: `npm test` **1,257/1,257** في **181/181** ملفًا (+8 اختبارات العقد، +2 لعيادة الخطأ، ثم +9 مع جيل الإصلاح: يوم الدراسة المحلي، سلامة الحالة المخزَّنة، النقل المؤجَّل، المصطلح الأجنبي في العربية — بلا تخفيف عتبة ولا تعطيل ملف) · typecheck نظيف · lint 0 أخطاء / 6 تحذيرات قائمة · `npm run build` 322/322 صفحة · بصمة `8f1f595b42f9` · full 5,876,583 · JS 124 chunks / 1,894,105 gzip / max 260,759 · الصوت 544 ملفًا / 52,943,843 bytes · المنهج 1,045,989 gzip · `workflows:check` و`handoff:check` يمرّان.
- عقد الجودة التحريرية: `boilerplateTailItems 0` · `duplicateExplanationPairs 0` · `maxRepeatedFinalSentence 10` بسقف 24 · `allUnder60Chars 0` بلا حشو — الأرقام من `reports/lesson-quality-audit.json` المولَّد، لا من الرواية.
- حدود صادقة: طول السطر حدّ أدنى آلي لا تقييم تربوي، ولا مراجعة لغوية بشرية للشرح؛ وأرقام المتصفح من CI وحده؛ PR #4 ما زال مفتوحًا والدمج قرار المالك.

## حصة العلاج في جلسة المراجعة وساعة الاستحقاق — 2026-10-03 (ADR-105، م36)

- عطلان مقاسان من المصدر: `dailyReviewQuota` يستثني صراحةً أحداث `personal-error-remediation` من عدّاد اليوم، و`eligibleReviewCards` يلحق كل بطاقات الأخطاء المؤكَّدة بالطابور بلا سقف — فلعلاج الأخطاء لا يُحصى ولا يُحدَّد، والحارس الوحيد `dueReviews >= 20` في `coach.ts` و`report.ts` يوقف الاقتراح ولا يحدّ الجلسة. والثاني: `timeZone` كان يُمرَّر إلى `calculateSM2` بلا `reviewHourLocal`، فتُثبَّت كل مواعيد الاستحقاق على منتصف الليل المحلي بدل ساعة المراجعة المختارة (18:00 افتراضيًا)، وقد يُدفع التاريخ يومًا كاملًا بلا سبب يراه المتعلم.
- سياسة `review-session-dose-v1` (`src/core/review/session-dose.ts`) تسقف **حصة العلاج وحدها** عند `max(1, ceil(quotaRequired / 2))`: لا تحذف بطاقة مجدولة، ولا تعيد ترتيب الطابور، ولا تغيّر `dailyReviewQuota` ولا الإتقان. عدّاد الجلسة يمنع استبدال البطاقة المنظورة بأخرى، و«جلسة أطول اليوم» يرفع السقف لهذه الجلسة فقط بطلب صريح. رقم `.review-count` يبقى عدد المستحق كله: لا إنكار لطابور ولا دَين مخفي.
- صفحة `/review` تعرض للمتعلّم سطرًا واحدًا «حصة العلاج في هذه الجلسة N من M» وحالة نهائية عند بلوغ السقف بخيار واحد وسبب مكتوب؛ والساعة المحلية صارت تُقرأ من تذكير المراجعة وتُمرَّر إلى SM-2 فتُثبت `ReviewItem.calendarTimeZone`/`reviewHourLocal` فعليًا.
- قياس ذلك الجيل: `npm test` **1,221/1,221** في **175/175** ملفًا (+11 اختبارًا لسياسة الحصة، بلا تخفيف عتبة ولا تعطيل ملف) · typecheck نظيف · lint 0 أخطاء / 6 تحذيرات قائمة · `language:audit:write` ثبّت 7430 وسمًا افتتاحيًا في 194 ملف TSX · `npm run build` 322/322 صفحة · بصمة `0d2add3b1207` · full 5,761,228 · JS 124 chunks / 1,881,894 gzip / max 266,168 · الصوت 544 ملفًا / 52,943,843 bytes · المنهج 1,045,989 gzip · `workflows:check` يمرّ.
- حدود صادقة: السقف قرار تصميمي لا نتيجة دراسة على متعلمين، فلا يُدَّعى أنه «أفضل» ولا أن المتعلمين راضون. أرقام المتصفح تُنسب إلى CI وحده وقد قيس هناك على هذا Head: `check` 2m48s و`e2e` 16m28s بنجاح لمشروعي desktop وmobile (run `37105579225`)؛ و`Deployment Smoke` على تشغيل الـPR كان skipping بحكم الحارس، أما فحص الإنتاج فلم يُعَد تشغيله بعد (PR #4 ما زال مفتوحًا والدمج قرار المالك).



## ما بعد الدمج: فحص النشر وعقد التدريس — 2026-10-02 (ADR-103 وADR-104)

- PR #3 أُدمج في `main` عند 2026-10-02T21:23:59Z (head `eb9c0616`) ونُشر إلى Production. فحص `Deployment Smoke` (run 37066936703، environment `Production`) **فشل** عند «Verify deployed critical routes and headers»؛ لم نصفه أبدًا «ناجحًا باستثناء».
- السبب المقاس: `environment_url` كان مضيف Vercel الخاص بالنشر `https://wegberlin-8u19akx07-...vercel.app` المحمي بتسجيل الدخول، فأُعيد التوجيه إلى `vercel.com/login` وفُسِّر ذلك كانكسار تطبيق (7/7 مسارات: هوية مفقودة، CSP مفقود، nosniff مفقود).
- الآن `scripts/smoke-deployment.mjs` وحدةٌ قابلة للاستيراد تفرّق ثلاث حالات: `passed` / `failure` / `blocked-by-auth`. تحدي المصادقة (401/403 أو تحويل إلى مزوّد هوية) لا يمر ولا يُسمّى عطل تطبيق، بل «لم يصل الفحص إلى التطبيق». يعطي Workflow تفضيل `vars.DEPLOYMENT_SMOKE_PRODUCTION_URL` على `environment_url`، ويكتب سبب الإعاقة إلى `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`. لا تجاوز لحماية Vercel ولا قراءة أسرار.
- إصلاحات عقد التدريس: تهيئة الملف الشخصي بلا مفاتيح إتقان وهمية (`mastery: {}`)؛ أدلة القراءة صار لكل سؤال دليل مؤسَّس على جملة فعلية من النص (verbatim / morphological / computed) أو إعلان صريح «سؤال استنتاجي» بلا الاستشهاد بجملة عشوائية؛ مهمة الإنتاج المؤجل تذكّر بعنوان الدرس المصدر ومستواه لا بمعرّف خام؛ وبوابة مراحل الدرس (`lesson-stage-work-gate-v1`) تمنع الدخول إلى الاختبار القصير قبل محاولة تدريبية فعلية في كل موجّه والقراءة والاستماع، وتشترط عملًا مسجّلًا لإغلاق مرحلة تطلب عملًا. البوابة بوابة تنقّل فقط: لا تغيّر الإكمال ولا الإتقان ولا تضيف حالة محفوظة.
- قياس ذلك الجيل: `npm test` **1,210/1,210** في **174/174** ملفًا (+10 اختبارات جديدة، بلا تخفيف عتبة ولا تعطيل ملف) · lint 0 أخطاء / 6 تحذيرات قائمة · TypeScript typecheck نجح · `npm run build` 322/322 صفحة · `npm run handoff:check` نجح.
- بصمة البناء في ذلك الجيل: 329d37aa1324 · ef613e05d712 · 4ee51eb6c820 · full 5,759,934 · JS 124 chunks / 1,880,826 gzip / max 266,168 · الصوت 544 ملفًا / 52,943,843 bytes · المنهج 1,045,989 gzip. المنهج والبيانات والصوت لم تتغير؛ تغيّر الترويسات فقط في تقارير البناء المولّدة.
- حدود صادقة: مجموعة Playwright **لم تُعَد في هذه البيئة** (لا binary لـChromium ولا وصول إلى CDN)، فأرقام 58/58 تخص جيل ما قبل الدمج ولا تُنسب لهذه التغييرات؛ إعادة تشغيل Smoke الإنتاجي مستحيلة هنا (لا egress إلى *.vercel.app)؛ يبقى مطلوبًا أن يثبّت المالك `DEPLOYMENT_SMOKE_PRODUCTION_URL` على الاسم المستعار العام ثم يُعيد تشغيل الـworkflow. **(تحديث 2026-10-04، ADR-109: لم يعد ذلك شرطًا — الاسم المستعار العام صار الافتراضي داخل الـworkflow؛ المتغير تجاوز اختياري.)** لا ادعاء مراجعة بشرية لغوية أو حقوقية، ولا تقييم صوتي يفوق ما تثبته المحركات، ولا «بديل أفضل» بلا متعلمين حقيقيين، ولا ضمان نتيجة امتحان.

## إصلاح مسارات ESM لـWebGPU — 2026-10-02 (ADR-102)

- أُعيد إنتاج رسالة `Failed to resolve module specifier "onnxruntime-web/webgpu"` في Chromium 153.0.8010.0 قبل التغيير. ملف Transformers.js 4.2.0 المتصفحّي كان يترك أيضًا `onnxruntime-common` كـbare import، فكان فشل حل الوحدة يسبق إنشاء pipeline.
- صار materializer يتحقق من ملف Transformers الأصلي وبصمة npm الثابتة لحزمة `onnxruntime-web@1.26.0-dev.20260416-b7804b056c`، ثم يعيد كتابة المرجعين المحددين فقط إلى حزمة WebGPU محلية واحدة، ويتحقق من hashes المصدر والمخرجات. لا تغيير للنموذج أو `device: webgpu` أو تنزيل الأوزان أو موافقة المستخدم أو سياسة الخصوصية/البديل.
- نجاح `npm run build`: 322/322 صفحة، 123 chunk، وميزانيات Offline/JS/الصوت اجتازت. نجاح `npm test`: 1,198/1,198 في 173/173 ملفًا. اختبار Chromium native module Worker: 1/1 desktop و1/1 mobile، استورد الملفين الفعليين وبنى Tensor ولم يطلب أوزان Hugging Face.
- هذا يثبت تحميل ESM فقط؛ لم تُنزّل أوزان ولا جرى استدلال فعلي على WebGPU، لذلك لا ندّعي نجاحًا على GPU حقيقي. تظل مراجعة الأجهزة/التوافق الفيزيائي معلقة.

## Current milestone
Phase 5 — core product, portability, Offline pack, continuous exam rehearsal, bilingual search, full generated audio, evidence-gated lessons, evidence-derived analytics, and delayed error remediation are complete. All **96/96** A1–B2 lessons (24 per level, measured from `academicLessonList`) and all 80/80 library listening items have generated MP3 assets. Synthetic exam-audio production is now complete: 96 verified physical MP3 files cover all 90/90 logical clips, all 7/7 targeted listening tasks, and all 35/35 full-simulation listening tasks across Goethe and telc. Long clips play as ordered segment sequences under one persisted play limit. Exam-audio manifest v2 audits all 42/42 listening tasks and reports zero partial or missing tasks. `docs/AUDIO_PRODUCTION_BACKLOG.md` now records the completed production state. Human/multi-speaker voice review, rights review, and independent academic review remain.

## Generation v187 (2026-09-29) — **P2-215 closed**: an optional second opinion, compared side by side and never merged

- **Executed (code + tests + docs).** `src/core/writing/review-comparison.ts` — policy `review-disagreement-comparison-v1`:
  after an advisory Gemini review exists, the learner may request **one** optional second pass with a declared angle
  (`coverage` · `style`) over the *same* free BYOK path, each with its **own** consent dialog. The comparison then shows
  **what both reviews agree on · what only one saw · and where they really disagree** (same excerpt, different suggestion;
  or same suggestion, different confidence) — with `noScore`, `noMajorityVote`, `noAutoPick`, `noThirdRequest` pinned in the
  type and re-checked by `assertReviewComparisonIntegrity`. `needsHumanReview` is raised **only** by a real disagreement.
- **Field moved, honestly.** New nested evidence field `comparisonSlot` (`first`/`second`, optional) on stored reviews; it
  survives `.dwnb` export/import because the portability schema names it — no top-level state field was added, so the
  66-field classification (19/25/12/10) is unchanged.
- **Pointer pins refreshed (caught by the gate, not hidden).** The new panel block carries JSX ⇒ `source:audit --strict`
  refused stale artifacts ⇒ `language:audit:write` moved the counters to **189 TSX / 7,496 opening tags / 416 German
  fragments / 49 technical-scoped / 0 issues** (SHA `5e6c3dc0fe95…`), re-pinned in the unit test, ADR-019 and the verifier.
- **Row closure.** `P2_AUDIT` row **215 moves `not-implemented` ⇒ `implemented`**: counters
  **implemented 128 ⇒ 129 · partial 2 · not-implemented 12 ⇒ 11 · blocked 0 (142 rows)**; the prompt's open list is now
  `276, 349, 350, 352, 355, 357, 359, 360, 371, 400, 401`.
- **New docs.** ADR-099 · run log `docs/run-logs/2026-09-29-review-disagreement-comparison/`.
- **Gates.** `npm run check` exit **0**: lint 0 (6 standing warnings, 0 errors) · tsc 0 · **1,155 unit/integrity tests in 171 files**
  (169.0 s) · `secret:audit` (0 findings) · `source:audit --strict` · `handoff:check` 0 · build **321/321 pages**
  (compile 10.1 s, static generation 8.8 s) · `offline:size` **`803cfb9bf4fa`** (a1 2,479,857 · a2 2,609,758 · b1 2,623,164 ·
  b2 3,985,466 · **full 5,787,117**) · `js:budget` 110 / 1,800,823 / 250,914 · `media:budget` 544 / 52,943,843 bytes /
  curriculum 975,094 gzip · pack cache `dwnb-full-pack-v179` (staging/previous v178).
- **Wipe #11 (same recurring tree revert).** The window opened with the tree back at 160 test files (no
  `exam-partner-simulation.ts`, no `node_modules`): `sha256sum -c` on the delivery zip was **OK**, the archive was extracted
  in full (2,043 entries → 170 test files) and dependencies were rebuilt (`npm ci` + Playwright install + install-deps, all
  exit 0). No selective restore was used.
- **Honest build note (three real failures, all reported).** After the wipe the first build attempt was refused by the
  language audit (expected, fixed above); the next was **OOM-killed / thrashing** with the build's type-check child at
  ~750–805 MB on this 1.98 GB host with no swap, and a third with a 704 MB heap died as a **V8 heap** failure — the checker
  needs about a gigabyte. What actually unblocked it: refresh the incremental type cache (`tsc --noEmit` clean first) and run
  the build on a quiet host with the standing 1024 MB heap ⇒ **`next build --webpack` exit 0, 321/321 pages, TypeScript
  phase 51 s**. No check was disabled, no cap raised, no timeout changed.
- **Browser (v187).** Measured on build `803cfb9bf4fa`, suite still **52 tests per project** (the P2-215 work extended the

## Generation v158 (2026-09-23) — woven nursing layer

- **The owner-contract nursing layer is built (ADR-080 §4, ADR-085).** `nursing-layer-v1` adds **14 woven units** (A1 4 · A2 4 · B1 3 · B2 3), each inside an existing lesson's rule stage: "توابل" at A1/A2 reframes level material inside a care situation with **no professional word at all** (budget 0/0), "طبق ثانٍ" at B1+ carries **8 of 8** declared professional words (budget 4/4). Every unit names the theory blocks it reuses, so no unit may introduce grammar above its level.
- **The three strict rules are measured, not promised.** (a) every `reusedTheoryIds` entry must resolve inside its own lesson; (b) exactly one accepted action per task — `recognize`/`confirm`/`ask`/`document` — with an **obligatory guess option the environment fails** (22 such options), and accepted texts that read like dosing instructions are rejected; (c) a structural check counts layer importers (5 declared: registry, validator, practice core, panel, tests) and fails on any undeclared importer or on **any mention inside `src/core/{exams,lessons,evidence,review,assessment,diagnostic}` (0 hits)**, so deleting one registry file leaves the general B2 path complete.
- **Zero learning effect, proven from storage.** `masteryEffect/evidenceEffect/gateEffect = none` in the core, the panel and both tests; the browser test opens `a1-22`, fails a guess, accepts the language act and then reads IndexedDB to assert `mastery` stayed empty and no attempt was written. B1+ words leave through the *existing* personal-vocabulary TSV header (`German → Arabic → Example → Tags`) — the layer never writes storage itself.
- **Rollback breaker with a numeric trigger.** `nursing-layer-rollback-v1`: metric = general exam-shape task accuracy, window 14 days, trigger = a 5-percentage-point drop, action = reduce the layer and keep the certificate path; `layerRemovable: true`.
- **Disclaimer on every card.** "هذه صياغات لغوية لا إرشاد سريري" is mandatory field-by-field: the audit fails if a single unit loses it. Professional review debt is explicit and **not** diluted: 14 new units are `pending-nursing-professional` with **0 reviews**, and the layer's own report says so.
- **Gate wiring.** `npm run nursing:layer` (write/check) runs in `prebuild`; `handoff:check` pins the audit identity, per-level coverage, word budget, failed-guess count, zero couplings, the report/ADR/unit-test texts and the browser-flow selectors. The language/Bidi audit re-measured at this generation: **180 TSX / 7,046 tags / 406 German / 235 mixed static / 0 issues**, re-pinned in its test, ADR and the verifier.

- **Build (v158).** `npm run build` (Webpack) exit 0 · **321/321** pages; `offline:size` **ed3c75aebd86** (a1 2,422,337 · a2 2,541,422 · b1 2,565,697 · b2 3,918,897 · full 5,709,344) — this fingerprint hashes the measured bytes of the built route HTML and its discovered Next assets, so it is **build-scoped, not content-scoped**: two builds of this identical source measured 566f4883bbb8/5,710,883 and ed3c75aebd86/5,709,344 (tens of bytes apart, Next's build id lives in the HTML). The content identity that does not move is the Offline route manifest: 318 routes in packs 58/58/58/219/318, pack cache `dwnb-full-pack-v175` · `js:budget` 110 / 1,750,122 / 250,914 · `media:budget` 544 / 52,943,843 bytes / curriculum 969,384 gzip · **1,050 unit/integrity tests in 160 files** · tsc 0 · lint 0 · `handoff:check` 0 · pack `dwnb-full-pack-v175` (staging/previous v157) in seven files · 13 documents on `Sync batch: v175`. No assertion was weakened and no project was skipped. On mobile the same flow reaches the rule stage through the footer «أكملت هذه الخطوة» path, since `.lesson-steps` is `display:none` below 850 px, and it now carries an explicit `test.setTimeout(90_000)` like the other heavy flows. Build artifacts were unchanged by this test-only edit.

## Implemented
- `professional-guidance-navigation-v1` establishes the application-wide UX hierarchy: desktop routes are grouped into daily learning and resources/tools, the top bar names the current route context, and the mobile bar provides four frequent destinations plus an accessible all-route bottom sheet. The sheet has visible labels, current-location text, Escape/focus trap/return, no-progress-mutation wording, reduced motion, and 320 px overflow coverage. See ADR-062.
- ADR-063 closes P2-381/383. `virtual-german-character-keyboard-v1` mounts globally but appears only for editable German fields, inserting `ä ö ü ß Ä Ö Ü` at the selection without reading/storing/correcting/submitting text. `bilingual-grammar-glossary-v1` adds 24 authored terms balanced 6/6/6/6 across A1–B2 with Arabic definitions and German examples in a local searchable Search panel. Both are no-network/no-mastery support.
- ADR-064 closes P2-142. `adaptive-partial-full-dictation-v1` adds 16 original tasks balanced 4/4/4/4: A1 partial, A2 partial-to-full, B1 bridge-to-full, and B2 full. The guided prepare→listen→write→compare flow locks the model until commitment, distinguishes word edits from capitalization/punctuation-only differences, and supports penalty-free hidden-model retry. Strict `dictationAttempts` stores playback/form summary only, never learner answer text or mastery; Browser TTS remains synthetic, device-dependent, and non-exam-grade. `/practice/dictation` remains included in every Offline pack.
- ADR-065 closes P2-354. `offline-branching-conversation-v1` adds eight original deterministic trees balanced 2/2/2/2 across A1–B2. Role and communicative goal precede each branch; authored German choices lead to distinct progress, repair, partial, or restart consequences, with guided Arabic-intention and delayed-support challenge modes. Tree validation rejects cycles, missing nodes, unreachable nodes, and incomplete outcomes; attempt creation replays branch provenance before accepting a terminal. Strict `branchingConversationAttempts` stores choice IDs/outcome/support count only—no free text, audio, AI, live-partner claim, mastery, or CEFR score.
- ADR-066 closes P2-108. `contextual-collocation-network-v1` adds 16 original networks and 48 contextual links balanced 4/4/4/4 across A1–B2. Every link has German/Arabic situation, register, example, meaning, and a contrast boundary. Learners must explore all three links before a three-context German-first guided/challenge match. Strict `collocationNetworkAttempts` stores only authored IDs and count, without free text, auto-SRS, AI, mastery, or CEFR. `/practice/collocations` moves current Offline packs to 58/58/58/210/309 and synchronized caches to v118.
- ADR-067 closes P2-335. `deterministic-generative-properties-v1` adds a dependency-free seeded property harness with deterministic replay, bounded shrinking, and counterexample reporting. Eight tests execute 8,301 generated/replayed cases over German normalization, accepted comparison, special-character insertion, Merge union/max/idempotence, strict collocation counts, random conversation terminals, and Zod portability. The first run exposed `Straße→STRASSE`; the test preserves the intentional strict ß/ss boundary rather than weakening evaluation.
- ADR-068 closes P2-310. `vercel-docs-only-build-skip-v1` wires a dependency-free `ignoreCommand` into `vercel.json`: only root/authored-docs Markdown changes return Vercel's skip code. Runtime, package, public, tests, workflows, config, and generated governance docs always build. Empty/unavailable diffs, unsafe paths, Git errors, and timeouts fail open to a build; main deployment remains enabled.
- P0 official-source/free-tier governance is now operational. `src/config/source-verification-registry.json` contains 19/19 official source records (five exam-format, five remote-AI, seven browser-runtime/model/license, Vercel Hobby, and GitHub Actions), each with a stable ID, observed state, exact project claim, human-verification date, 30-day maximum age, and stale action. `npm run source:audit -- --strict` is part of `npm run check`; `.github/workflows/source-freshness.yml` runs monthly, probes reachability, and opens/updates one maintenance Issue when review is due. The Exam Hub renders current/due/stale source state, while the 0 USD guard blocks unknown Gemini models, non-Free-only OpenRouter IDs, and all stale remote-AI requests before `fetch`; Disabled/Ollama and the full local course remain available. HTTP success is explicitly not treated as semantic verification.
- `ZERO_COST.md`, `docs/SOURCE_FRESHNESS.md`, and ADR-007 record the current zero-cost boundaries, model allowlists, local fallbacks, anti-billing behavior, and human review checklist. Current verification is 2026-09-03 with the next deadline 2026-10-03; the Goethe HTML overview returns an anti-bot 403 to generic CI and is explicitly retained as a manual check while official PDFs remain probeable.
- `beginner-readable-completion-v1` and `guided-mediation-from-understanding-to-free-v1` start the real-learner UX repair. Comfortable typography is the default with compact/comfortable/larger controls in Settings and the top bar. The lesson gate now exposes one next action and a plain checklist rather than raw evidence fractions. `a1-01` speaking is reduced to 5–10 seconds, begins with synthetic phrase models, has no forced preparation timer, and keeps support visible by default; `supportVisibleDuringRecording` prevents guided attempts from counting as independent level-gate or progress evidence. `a1-01` mediation now starts with an aligned goal and source-grounded audience/purpose/facts. ADR-060 now adds word-level local STT separately; phoneme correction remains pending. See ADR-059.
- `local-german-word-matching-v1` adds the first actual local speech-recognition cycle. Settings checks HTTPS/WebGPU/4 GB/128 MiB/browser quota, source freshness, and low-data mode before an explicit 70–90 MB q8 Whisper tiny download into `dwnb-pronunciation-model-v1`. A dedicated Worker transcribes only 0.35–20 second German samples from its complete cache. `local-microphone-signal-check-v1` blocks silence, low level, and clipping before ASR without pretending to identify a noise source; the speaking lab matches authored target words, ignores extra names, shows at most three corrections, and turns one unconfirmed word into a complete listen→record→playback→signal-check→local-match→retry loop. Transcript/result stay in page memory, audio is never sent, and no score/mastery/phoneme/accent/fluency claim is created. Real device/thermal/crash and Arabic-learner acoustic validation remain under P0-255/P1-380. See ADR-060.
- P0 academic runtime schemas cover all published roots with 16 strict Zod families. `prebuild` validates 4,345 top-level objects, including 1,297 noun records, 134 verb-preposition frames, and 17 Tunisian notes, plus every nested object and cross-reference.
- P0 answer integrity now emits one machine-readable registry for 2,805 closed-answer items and 384 productive tasks. Every closed item links prompt, keyed answer, evidence reference/excerpt, and a type-aware visibility policy; direct prompt leakage has zero unapproved failures. Three explicit exemptions are recorded for two authentic repeated-greeting stimuli and one editing task that intentionally retains a neutral clause. Productive tasks remain `no-single-answer` or `model-after-commit` rather than receiving fabricated keys.
- P0 objective coverage now emits a readable 389/389 objective map: A1 96, A2 96, B1 96, B2 101, each with teaching, practice, and Mini-Test references and zero structural gaps. The report states that lesson-scoped structural mapping does not replace human semantic alignment review. Committed reports share a SHA-256 and are checked for drift by `npm run content:audit`.
- `lexical-target-gap-v1` no longer reports empty queues: after the four final B2 lessons the inventory holds 4,685 noun signals (1,376 covered / 89 pending human / 3,220 context-only) and 1,090 verb-frame signals (134 covered / 4 unclassified / 952 not-target), plus eight structural exclusions awaiting independent confirmation. Nouns now stand at 1,244 and frames at 134. Non-zero queues are open human review work — the batch deliberately did not auto-close P0-99 or P1-380 — and covered rows are structural evidence, not independent German approval.
- P0 `novelty-weighting-v1` now makes evidence novelty explicit: first unseen transfer item weight 1.5, first guided item 1, and only the latest same-item retry 0.25 regardless of click count. Lesson completion mastery uses weighted accuracy plus unique-item coverage rather than counting every correct retry; evidence reports expose novel items, transfer items, retry attempts, weighting version, and the limited-weight boundary.
- P0 SM-2 calendar policy is now `sm2-v2-calendar` + `review-calendar-v1`. Scheduling adds intervals to the learner browser's IANA local calendar day and converts the target local boundary back to a UTC instant, preserving local review days across DST. Review items/events persist the policy and timezone; legacy `sm2-v1` records remain schema-valid. Tests cover UTC, Africa/Tunis, Europe/Berlin, America/New_York, Australia/Sydney, spring/fall behavior, six-day DST crossings, and invalid-zone fail-closed behavior.
- `PROFESSIONAL_CONTINUATION_PROMPT_AR.md` was re-audited on 2026-09-10 as a pasteable recovery prompt containing immutable product rules, exact current counters, implemented architecture, all 9 partial/0 missing/0 blocked P0 items, formal P1 audit, conservative P2 overlap, remaining themes, execution protocol, prohibited claims, recovery verification, and the safe Termux extraction/checksum/push command. It records P0-160 as implemented through an Offline deterministic question grounded in a learner-typed answer/transcript, forbids claiming that typed follow-up understands audio and forbids phoneme/fluency scoring, preserves per-send consent for optional network AI, records P0-219 as an opt-in browser WebGPU embedding model with capability/storage/license/delete boundaries, records P1-210/222/224 as linked consented Tutor commands plus an honest five-provider capability/mock matrix, records P1-139/150/151 as staged listening plus original articulation/contrast practice, records P1-175/186 as actual-writing repair plus practice/law classification, records P1-271/272 as evidence achievements plus fully optional gamification, records P1-338/341 as safe Review shortcuts plus local notes, records P1-344/366 as safe TSV vocabulary plus local unsubmitted reports, and records the current P1 audit at 128 implemented / 4 partial / 0 not implemented / 0 blocked. `P2_AUDIT.md` now provides the first formal 140/140 row audit: 118 implemented / 2 partial / 20 not implemented / 0 blocked; these counts do not supersede P0/P1 human-review boundaries.
- `TERMUX_REPLACE_REPO.sh`, `TERMUX_GITHUB_UPLOAD.md`, and copy-ready `TERMUX_ONE_COMMAND.txt` standardize on `wegberlin-full.zip` plus its `.sha256` sidecar. - GitHub Quality Gate run 3 exposed a remote e2e failure while its `check` and production Build jobs passed. The workspace workflow now uses checkout/setup-node v5, runs both desktop and mobile projects, raises the timeout to 25 minutes, and allows one retry only under `CI`; local runs remain retry-free so defects are not hidden. The current retry-free production run passed 41/41 desktop and 41/41 mobile, including CSP response headers, no-blame time comparison, readiness ranges, selective JSON download, and direct `/offline` navigation. Every row records the actual evidence and the exact remaining criterion; this replaces the former overlap-only list and permits P1 numbers without implying human academic review.
- `P2_AUDIT.md` classifies all 142/142 P2 proposals: 118 implemented, 2 partial, 22 not implemented, and 0 blocked. 44 formerly partial software rows, including P2-273, are now closed. - P2 `event-derived-mastery-v1` closes P2-72/237. The central LearningProvider captures every new runtime mutation of `mastery` as a strict set/increment/delete event with source, previous/resulting value, and bounded evidence references; the materialized record is a recalculated cache. Merge unions stable event IDs before reduction, so distinct concurrent delayed-review increments accumulate, duplicate IDs do not double count, later sets can lower an older score, and delete events prevent workflow markers from reappearing. Old snapshots remain explicitly labeled Legacy fallback until new evidence exists; Zod, IndexedDB, DWNB, partial export, import normalization, Merge, Progress UI, and tests preserve the contract.
- P2 `guided-independent-support-separation-v1` closes P2-70/287. It maps authored lesson items to guided controlled practice or transfer, selects only the latest unique attempt, and marks an attempt assisted only when a direct same-item support event occurred before commitment. Post-commit translation/transcript access and help on another item never contaminate prior independent evidence. Progress exposes guided, transfer-independent, transfer-assisted, and total assisted/independent counts and accuracies without copying answers, scoring productive language, or changing mastery.
- P2 `bounded-attempt-process-v1` closes P2-33/34. ExerciseCard and QuestionQuiz capture visible response time excluding hidden-page duration, discrete committed selection revisions, and an optional German-first learner attribution (`knowledge-recall`, `guess`, or `instruction-unclear`). Text inputs start timing but never log individual keystrokes or intermediate text. Progress summarizes median time, revisions, direct pre-commit help, and uncertainty kinds as planning context only; values are capped, strict-Zod validated, DWNB/Merge portable, and cannot change correctness, CEFR, or mastery.
- P2 `explainable-plan-change-timeline-v1` closes P2-48 with a compact Today history derived from low-energy/time check-ins, immediate adaptations, equivalent alternatives, learner decisions on automatic load offers, next-focus choices, and learning-contract revisions. Every row states a simple reason and optional before→after minutes; it excludes answer text and free reflection text, creates no mastery or penalty, and does not manufacture an event when no plan change exists.
- P2 `learner-selected-intensity-presets-v1` closes P2-57 with explicit Light/Balanced/Intensive choices in Settings. Light never exceeds 20 minutes, Balanced equals the latest contract/profile base, and Intensive advances exactly one valid 10/20/30/45/60/90 step only after learner selection. Check-in, low energy, prior lighter focus, and immediate reduction retain priority. The preference affects Today and future weekly budgets, is shown in the plan-change timeline, and is Zod/DWNB/Merge compatible without mastery or penalty.
- P2 daily-focus production closes P2-45/47/201/239/286/347 with persisted morning/evening entry modes, a learner pin that preserves the coach recommendation, five-minute weakest-evidence practice, 12/8/4/1 countdown guidance, local +30-minute what-if, and level-end DWNB reminders. Existing local voice selection closes P2-153 and printable answer sheets close P2-203.
- P2 partial-closure sprint closes 44 formerly partial software rows. The implemented contracts cover independent weekly reflection, ten-minute rescue, fatigue resume, target-date load/risk, optional rituals, four-skill phrase recycling, writing-derived vocabulary preview, error spans, style/context choice, interest-filtered library, adversarial library questions, direct summaries/responses, rhythm taps, best recording, separated rubrics, browser correction-off writing, printable portfolio and bounded writing patterns/100 words, consented live model descriptors, Opus-first recording, low-data mode, global shortcuts, exam print sheets, raw-store export, delivery health, deployment smoke, 15% JS/media budgets, threat/redaction, Arabic privacy, pairwise migrations, global German copy, local voice/pitch, learner-trained local classifier, multi-step practical day, unified concept map, previous-pack rollback, proposal gate, and meaning-translation audit. P2-264 and P2-384 remain partial for real assistive-technology/WCAG review and independent Arabic regional review; automation does not close them.

Pack audio counts are 40/48/48/124/260; `media-pack-budget-v1` enforces curriculum, combined audio, and per-pack ceilings with a mandatory 15% reserve.
- P1-235/246/247/248 are documented in ADR-049: `partial-study-sections-export-v1` emits only selected non-restorable review JSON; `/offline` is a dedicated recovery route; the Service Worker resumes only checksum-verified completed resources from staging; and `js-budget-v1` fails postbuild above the committed Gzip limits. None is represented as byte-range resume, a full backup, or physical performance proof.
- P1-282/284/318/365 are implemented in ADR-050. `weekly-planned-actual-no-blame-v1` compares only elapsed plan minutes with recorded activity estimates and never creates penalty/debt/mastery. `evidence-velocity-readiness-range-v1` shows a provider-scoped week range or insufficient-data without a pass date. `vercel-csp-headers-v1` centralizes 15 directives and 7 response headers with no global wildcard/generic unsafe-eval and a build audit. `dwnb-deprecation-policy-v1` supports v1 with a runtime warning through 2027-03-31, keeps v2/v3 current, migrates old state before strict validation, and rejects expired/unknown formats explicitly before mutation.
- ADR-051 closes P1-367/368/389. `independent-curriculum-version-v1` stores curriculum `dwnb-a1-b2-2026.09-v1` separately from app `0.1.0` in state, DWNB, migration, Merge, and preview. `content-accountability-lifecycle-v1` assigns distinct owner/reviewer roles and Draft→Validated→Published evidence to all 3,020 review records across 16 families, all 18 external sources, and all 12 runtime learner-risk types. The generated audit fails on any gap; publication remains separate from independent review.
- `general-consular-legal-claims-v1` now contains 12 general, lesson-linked, `officialClaimStatus:not-claimed` communication rows across six contexts with no fabricated authority source; actual specialist review remains pending, so P1-377 stays partial. `arabic-learner-pronunciation-inventory-v1` contains 18 `notUniversal:true` contrasts mapped to lessons and diagrams, 11 with exact existing TTS pairs; seven pairs and independent phonetics review remain, so P1-380 stays partial.
- P1 `journey-state-machine-v1` makes the learner journey explicit on `/today`: Orientierung → Grundlage → Aufbau → Festigung → Prüfungsreife. The phase, percentage, reason, transition condition, and non-official evidence boundary are derived from onboarding/diagnostic state, 84 lesson completions, A1–B2 gates, and provider-scoped exam readiness. Browsing cannot advance it.
- P1 `session-adaptation-v1` closes P1-7/8 after Check-in. “Less time” selects the nearest valid lower budget without going below completed blocks plus reflection; “too easy” transfers at most three minutes from unfinished guided practice to production; “too hard” caps the plan conservatively. Every record keeps reason, before/after minutes, already-completed block IDs, and `planning-signal-no-mastery-or-correctness`; mastery, correctness, and completed evidence are untouched. DWNB and daily-session merge preserve provenance.
- P1 `reading-comprehension-benchmark-v1` closes P1-126. A level-matched original library text is timed only after explicit Start and only while visible; hiding the page cancels without evidence. The text disappears before two questions, and WPM is omitted unless comprehension is 2/2. Qualified WPM is capped at 400, maps to 5/6/8/10 minutes, and splits the existing lesson budget into a reading block without increasing Today or mastery. Attempts are deletable and portable.
- P1 `writing-device-benchmark-v1` completes P1-18 inside the existing beginner-safe Writing Lab. It reveals a level-specific original copy sentence only after Start, blocks paste, cancels when hidden, and requires 90% copy similarity plus 80% target length before storing capped WPM/CPM. The values measure device input, not German quality. A 5/8/10/12-minute writing block is carved from the existing practice/production pool without changing total time, mastery, writing feedback, or `writingSubmissions`; evidence is deletable and DWNB/merge portable.
- P1 `accessibility-preferences-v1` closes P1-19/258. - Next.js 16 strict TypeScript application with 321 generated static/SSG pages, including 96 lesson routes, four level gates, 150 exam-task routes, twelve full-simulation dashboards, and the dedicated mediation lab.
- RTL Arabic responsive shell and guidance-first `/today` home.
- P0 keyboard/screen-reader navigation layer: a first-tab skip link targets a programmatically focusable `<main>`, every lesson-stage change moves focus to its bilingual heading, the active stage exposes `aria-current="step"`, and the 14-stage bar exposes real progressbar values. Exercise/reading/listening/Mini-Test feedback and closed-exam submission use polite atomic status regions instead of relying on color or visual insertion alone.
- `status-announcement-v1` now generalizes results without noisy repetition: visible text is separate from one hidden polite atomic live node, whitespace is normalized, unchanged rerenders are ignored, and only changed messages advance the announcement sequence. Diagnostic, all four level gates, module/error results, writing/speaking/mediation, all targeted exam result families, continuous submission, Review, Today, and Settings use the policy. This closes automated P0-256 while physical assistive-technology review remains P0-255.
- `language-boundary-v1` now audits all 138 TSX files and 5,284 opening tags during `prebuild`: 273 explicit German fragments pair language/direction, regional Arabic tags such as `ar-TN` require RTL, technical values own 27 explicit scopes, 10 generic answer renderers classify the actual string, and 185 Arabic+Latin static nodes use plaintext/isolate containment. Raw bidi controls and unpaired directions fail the build.
- `tunisian-support-v1` makes the onboarding preference materially affect the rule stage. A versioned registry provides 17 optional notes in 17 lessons, split A1/A2/B1/B2 = 6/5/4/2 across 15 contrast categories. Every note separates an MSA bridge, short Tunisian approximation, comprehension/transfer risk, and German anchor; MSA and minimal-Arabic modes render none. `tunisian:audit` verifies all 17 published theory references, exclusive visibility, non-duplicate text, and review-evidence consistency. All records remain `authored-review-pending`, so P0-373/376 stay partial until the final independent Tunisian/MSA review.
- All destructive/consent dialogs now share one accessible modal primitive: initial focus enters the first safe control, Tab and Shift+Tab remain trapped, Escape cancels, title/description relationships are explicit, and focus returns to the opening control after close. Unit and production-browser tests exercise forward/backward trapping, Escape, and restoration.
- P0 onboarding for prior German experience, exam, target date, daily time, Arabic support, and one-or-more learner goals. `priorExperience=none` is the safe default: it bypasses placement and routes directly to `/lernen/a1-01` with a capped 30-minute check-in→lesson→reflection session and no writing/production demand. Learners selecting some/unsure retain the adaptive diagnostic. The optional MP3/microphone check remains skippable and all new fields are local, backup-compatible, and optional for older profiles.
- P0 adaptive A1–B2 diagnostic with two independent 16-item forms. Each level has one grammar, vocabulary, reading, and generated-MP3 listening item; the engine asks 4–16 questions, advances only at 3/4, stops when the level boundary appears, alternates A/B on retest, stores attempted/correct evidence by level and skill plus sample confidence, extracts actionable errors, and explicitly avoids treating spelling as comprehension.
- `diagnostic-productive-sample-v1` now follows the closed-question boundary for experienced/uncertain learners: one German-first prompt accepts at least three written words, a 3–45 second local recording, both, or explicit no-penalty `not-yet`. It stores self-assessed independence and optional media under `self-evidence-no-automated-language-score`, never changes receptive placement, and has no language/pronunciation/CEFR score. Diagnostic media round-trips through DWNB, namespaces on isolated import, and participates in immediate deletion.
- `meaning-first-case-v1` inserts a learner-facing Bedeutung → Rolle → Form sequence before theory in 17 case-teaching lessons. The registry maps 21 theory blocks, 54 controlled exercises, and 40 Mini-Test items across A1–B2. A separate hashed `case:audit` runs in `prebuild`, owns every explicit case-name signal, and rejects form terminology in the semantic-first question, missing layers, or broken references.
- P0 coach session composition now produces exact 10/20/30/45/60/90-minute plans; every normal template includes check-in, independent production, and reflection. `pre-srs-retrieval-warmup-v1` now keeps the review allocation as a three-item German-first, delayed-reveal warm-up whenever no SRS card is eligible; it stores only mission completion and cannot create correctness, mastery, or retention evidence. The production block uses the learner's first non-exam goal to choose a work, study, daily-life, or settlement context without changing the locked curriculum order.
- Evidence report engine covering reading, listening, grammar/vocabulary, writing, and speaking. Receptive metrics use the latest unique attempt rather than retry volume; productive metrics expose task/version/recording coverage without pretending to judge language quality; overall score remains blank until at least three skills have evidence. A separate P0 error-rate indicator reports wrong checks per 100 recorded items together with the raw wrong/total denominator; it explicitly includes retries and does not pretend to analyze free-writing errors per 100 words.
- `/progress` now displays real sample sizes, correct counts, lesson/task coverage, confidence, boundaries, actual due SRS cards, reviewed-card coverage, active/repeated errors plus waiting/due delayed retests, grace-aware continuity, prioritized risks, and a linked next remediation action. The former hard-coded 12/8/4/3 skill percentages are removed.
- `/today` now uses the real local date, journey day, rolling seven-day history, evidence-derived streak/risk/coach note, and date-scoped mission completion IDs. Its P0 check-in records real available time and 1–5 energy; low energy caps the plan at 20 minutes. Its closing reflection records difficulty, unaided confidence, an optional note, and a next-focus decision (`continue`, `review`, `lighter`, or `production`). A prior `lighter` decision affects the next day until a fresh check-in overrides it.
- The P0 weekly planner builds Monday–Sunday locally from the configured budget and actual check-ins: six bounded study days plus deliberate Sunday rest, fixed writing on Wednesday, speaking on Thursday, exam practice on Saturday, and review on Monday/Friday. Sunday remains a zero-budget rest day unless the learner explicitly checks in; that voluntary low-energy session becomes a real bounded Today plan instead of disappearing from the weekly view. Every day's slots sum exactly to its budget. Under `weekly-grace-v1`, the most recent missed day is visibly marked as one grace day with no fabricated evidence or recovery debt. Additional missed days can move only one recovery slot into the next available day's existing budget; the remainder stays visible rather than doubling tomorrow.
- `behavioral-praise-v1` centralizes observable-action feedback across Today, Review, Writing, Speaking, and exam submission. It names retrieval-before-reveal, revision, listening back, honest repair, submission, or return after grace; tests reject generic applause such as «أحسنت/رائع/ممتاز/برافو».
- P0 `secret-audit-v1` now scans the current working tree inside `npm run check`, while Quality Gate uses `fetch-depth: 0` and requires a complete reachable-history scan before the normal check. `.githooks/pre-commit` is versioned and activated by the Termux replacement flow, which also runs the history gate before staging. A clean public clone on 2026-09-10 passed 30 commits and 49,812 changed text patch lines with zero findings after an exact-equality exemption for the intentional historical `sk-or-v1-EXAMPLEEXAMPLEEXAMPLE` fixture; neighboring values remain findings, matches are redacted, and real exposure still requires provider-side rotation.
- 84-lesson curriculum registry, now backed one-to-one by 84 complete academic lesson objects.

- Complete A2 level: Modules 1–8, lessons `a2-01` to `a2-24`, adding past narration, neighborhood, work, travel, health, media literacy, learning strategy, celebrations, volunteering, relative clauses, and group decisions.
- A2 final 48-item balanced knowledge assessment, module-gap report, productive-evidence criteria, A1 prerequisite, and guarded B1 readiness gate.
- Complete B1 level: Modules 1–8, lessons `b1-01` to `b1-24`, adding operational goal systems, `ohne … zu/ohne dass`, technical due diligence with Pronominaladverbien, and an integrated B1 evidence project.
- B1 final 48-item balanced assessment with A2 prerequisite, five B1 writing samples, five B1 speaking samples, and a guarded B2 gate.
- Complete B2 level: Modules 1–6, lessons `b2-01` to `b2-12`, culminating in timed productive work, a provider-neutral internal rehearsal, evidence-based remediation, and strict separation of Goethe and telc formats.
- B2 final 48-item balanced assessment with B1 prerequisite, six B2 writing samples, six B2 speaking samples, twelve completed lessons, and an internal readiness gate that does not claim official certification.

- Coach target engine enforces both A1 and A2 evidence gates before recommending B1.
- Writing and speaking labs receive the exact lesson task through a static client query adapter; writing now derives the minimum word target from each lesson prompt.
- Cumulative 10-item review and integrated writing/speaking transfer project for all completed modules.
- A1 final 48-item balanced knowledge assessment, module-gap report, productive-evidence criteria, and guarded A2 readiness gate.
- Coach target engine now selects the first unfinished lesson, prioritizes overdue SRS, enforces the A1 evidence gate, and recommends A2 only after readiness.
- Generic interactive exercise engine for MCQ, fill-blank, word ordering, error correction, and matching. Every controlled card is now German-first (`Wählen`, `Ergänzen`, `Ordnen`, `Korrigieren`) with immediate Arabic support. Learner-facing cards no longer expose internal IDs or raw type slugs; MCQ cards render their German stem, fill-blank templates replace invisible-looking underscores with a bordered `?` slot, and answer fields name the expected output.
- `docs/CONTENT_COMPLETENESS_AUDIT.md` records the exhaustive runtime audit: zero empty authored strings/non-finite numbers/release placeholders across 85 lessons, 160 library items, 32 diagnostic questions, 150 exam tasks, twelve dashboards, profiles, sources, and SRS content. It separately verifies 595 controlled surfaces, 924 lesson German questions, 320 library German questions, and the physical/logical audio contracts without equating automation with human review.
- Evidence-gated lesson completion across all 87 lessons: at least 70% unique correct controlled exercises, one unique correct reading item, one unique correct listening item, and 80% unique correct Mini-Test items. Repeating one correct item cannot inflate evidence; browsing remains open but the completion action stays disabled with a live 4-criterion report whose remediation buttons return directly to controlled practice, reading, listening, or Mini-Test.
- P0 delayed-support ladder now covers all five controlled exercise types and every lesson reading/listening/Mini-Test question. Two escalating hints teach a strategy or partial answer shape without exposing the complete expected answer; requesting a hint never creates correctness evidence. Unit tests enforce non-answer hints across every exercise type.
- Reading support is evidence-gated across all 96 lessons: glossary and Arabic translation are absent until the learner commits one reading answer. Every corrected reading item then shows a verbatim sentence selected from its source text by answer-weighted token overlap. All published questions are guaranteed a nonempty in-text quote, but the semantic choice is transparently algorithmic and still requires independent human review before being called authored evidence.
- P0 `lesson-shuffle-v1` applies a deterministic FNV-1a/Mulberry32 permutation to every controlled lesson MCQ and every reading/listening/Mini-Test option set. It forces a non-identity order, never mutates authored options, remaps correctness through the original index, and stores `answerIndex`, `shuffleSeed`, and `shuffleVersion` with each attempt. Exhaustive tests cover every published question and controlled MCQ so position changes cannot corrupt answer keys.
- Every lesson vocabulary stage now has a two-mode phrase deck: visible study or delayed active recall in German→Arabic and Arabic→German directions, honest remembered/retry grading, round reset, spoken German prompt, and a consolidated memory map for every grammar trick, pronunciation trick, and two high-priority error-prevention tricks.
- The four lexical registries render one German-first panel in all 96 lessons. Nouns are A1/A2/B1/B2 = 272/269/308/395, total 1,244; frames are 25/30/31/48, total 134. The machine queues are no longer zero — 89 noun candidates and 4 verb-frame candidates await independent human review, and nothing was auto-closed to make them disappear.
- Reading/listening quizzes, browser German TTS fallback, pronunciation playback, writing/speaking handoff, mediation, error clinics, and mini-tests.
- Deterministic SM-2 engine with an evidence-scoped queue: only cards from completed lessons are eligible; overdue cards precede new cards; future cards disappear from today's queue after grading; the next scheduled date and lesson/level source are shown; each card uses its own authored hint/trick. Re-finishing an already completed lesson no longer duplicates due counts or study-history evidence.
- P0 delayed-retention gate separates first exposure from evidence after time. Every grade appends an auditable `ReviewEvent` with card/lesson, scheduled and actual timestamps, grade, initial/delayed kind, and mastery delta. A new card always contributes zero mastery; only a successful already-scheduled due retrieval adds four points. The latest delayed result per card controls retention, so a later failure revokes that card's positive retention signal. Four distinct currently successful delayed cards are required before a lesson receives a visible retention sample, which is explicitly not called permanent mastery.
- Personal error notebook and P0 clustered clinics. Wrong lesson exercises and receptive/Mini-Test answers create stable readable records with answer, correction, explanation, timestamp, and category; repeats increment occurrences and reopen resolved records. At three active occurrences of one type, the system opens an automatic clinic that groups up to three source examples, teaches a type-specific rule and memory trick, contrasts wrong/correct models, and requires a new transfer sentence. Clinic attempts are stored locally and promoted into coach/progress risk priority, but clinic success never falsely resolves the individual errors. Individual treatment still requires immediate correct production and a second delayed recall; new wrong evidence resets repair progress. Umlaut/ß remains strict and phrase-recall self-grades are excluded.
- P0 writing lab now enforces five explicit phases: plan, draft, self-check, quoted feedback, and changed revision. It no longer has an isolated A1 fallback assignment: `/writing` resolves only to a lesson whose writing stage was reached or completed; otherwise an Arabic beginner-safe gate returns the learner to the current lesson without rendering the German prompt. Each reviewed version stores its plan, checklist, five indicators, feedback, and revision provenance; these are deterministic, not official or human scores.
- P0 speaking lab now enforces limited keyword preparation, hides phrases and notes during timed recording, requires full playback before saving, records achieved task criteria plus clarity/turn-taking/repair self-review, requires a specific retry reflection, compares duration only against the authored target, and links each improved attempt to its previous attempt. Audio and structured evidence stay in IndexedDB/DWNB; no acoustic, phoneme, pronunciation, or fluency score is claimed.
- Dedicated P0 `/mediation` lab over all 84 lesson tasks: identify audience/purpose and two source facts, produce an Arabic meaning transfer plus a German response when required, confirm anti-literal/completeness/constraints/audience/no-invention checks, receive five deterministic indicators with quoted evidence, reveal the authored comparison only after submission, and save a changed revision with source-version provenance. It never claims semantic equivalence or human assessment.
- P0 curriculum-grounded tutor now binds every request to the current lesson ID/level/first Can-Do objective and up to three bounded active-error records. It always renders a hint before a concise Arabic explanation, one-to-three German examples, and a new micro-exercise. The built-in disabled provider works without network; Gemini, OpenRouter Free-only, and a real Ollama `/api/chat` JSON path are optional.
- Every network-backed tutor request—including user-controlled Ollama—requires a fresh in-app consent that previews the exact question and destination before `fetch` can execute. Gemini requests JSON MIME output, OpenRouter is blocked unless the model is `openrouter/free` or ends in `:free`, and Ollama uses non-streaming JSON mode. Returned text is rejected unless it matches the strict four-field Zod contract; invalid or expanded payloads are neither rendered nor saved.
- Valid tutor interactions are stored locally with question, structured answer, provider, model, `tutor-v2` prompt version, lesson ID, referenced error IDs, consent evidence, and timestamp. The API key/endpoint stays only in `sessionStorage["dwnb-ai-key"]` and never enters state, DWNB, logs, or URLs. Tutor records round-trip through backup and merge deterministically.
- Separate, source-traceable Goethe/telc exam profiles with current official structure, timing, scoring boundaries, and explicit anti-mixing rules.
- P0 provider-scoped exam readiness is calculated separately for every module: Goethe Lesen/Hören/Schreiben/Sprechen and telc Leseverstehen/Sprachbausteine/Hörverstehen/Schreiben/Sprechen. Receptive modules use only that provider's latest task percentages plus a minimum sample threshold; writing uses unique submitted tasks and revisions, while speaking uses unique tasks and retries without fabricating accuracy. The hub shows coverage, sample requirement, status, remediation task, and weakest module—never one average that can hide a gap or mix official scoring systems. After the internal B2 gate, `/today` routes directly to that provider-owned weakest module; 20+ SRS reviews, due delayed errors, and three-occurrence clinics remain higher priorities. `/progress` becomes the hero target only after every module has strong internal evidence.
- Complete 24/24 original targeted-simulation contract with strict provider scope: Goethe 12/12 across reading parts 1–4, listening parts 1–4, writing parts 1–2, and speaking parts 1–2; telc 12/12 across all three reading, both Sprachbausteine, all three listening, writing, and all three speaking parts.
- Six complete original guided simulations for each provider. Every simulation owns an independent task bank; simulations 2–6 add 125 new task definitions while preserving Goethe's 5/4/2/2 and telc's 5/3/1/3 grouping.
- Full-session dashboards support two explicit modes: guided/resumable evidence review, or a fresh continuous-timed rehearsal. The latter stores a single absolute deadline (195 minutes for Goethe; 155 for telc), ignores evidence from older attempts, enforces provider-specific task order, advances only after a new submission, survives reload/profile backup, and blocks new submissions after expiry.
- P0 closed-assistance focus is now enforced on every active continuous dashboard/task route. The normal desktop/mobile navigation is removed and replaced by a compact focus bar; Arabic helper translations, format-source links, answer correction, explanations, listening transcripts, post-recording prompts, and speaking self-review are not rendered during the live attempt. Receptive/writing/speaking submission goes to a neutral next-task screen without revealing the score or key. A before-unload warning and in-app temporary-exit confirmation protect accidental departure without claiming browser lockdown; outside the focus route a persistent reminder links back while the absolute deadline continues.
- Continuous-session abandonment is explicit and separately confirmed. It changes the attempt to a non-resumable `abandoned` state, clears only unsubmitted task drafts and their temporary audio, preserves submitted mastery/writing/speaking evidence, preserves the old deadline for audit, and offers a fresh attempt with a new deadline. Completed, expired, and abandoned attempts never activate the focus shell, and provider/path ownership is tested to prevent cross-provider focus leakage.
- Continuous-session state is schema-validated, included in DWNB export/import and profile merging, and exposes a central clock on both the dashboard and every task. Per-task drafts preserve started state, receptive selections, listening play counters, debounced unfinished writing, and stopped speaking recordings across reloads; submission promotes temporary media to attempt evidence, removes the draft, and advances the sequence. Resetting the session deletes orphan drafts, while Import-as-new-profile namespaces their media IDs. It remains a local rehearsal rather than invigilation.
- `learning-playback-speed-v1` standardizes 0.75×/1×/1.15× across all authored listening surfaces: resilient onboarding/diagnostic audio, lesson listening, library listening, guided targeted/full exam listening, and Shadowing. Native audio receives `playbackRate`, `defaultPlaybackRate`, and `preservesPitch=true`; Browser TTS receives the same selected rate; ordered exam segments inherit it. Continuous timed rehearsal is visibly locked to 1×, and learner recordings are unaffected. Automated coverage is complete, while human distortion review remains P0-135 final-review work.
- `two-party-information-gap-v1` adds a genuine pass-and-play task to Speaking: private A/B facts, hidden handovers, four confirmed turns, a joint constraint-based decision, and no saved evidence without a real second-person confirmation. Solo rotation is rehearsal only.
- P0 `content-grounded-follow-up-v1` now adds an optional answer-grounded question after the learner records and listens back. The learner types a German summary/transcript; the default Offline engine deterministically uses an actual location, work/study, preference, plan/time, reason/opinion, or fallback content cue. Optional Gemini/OpenRouter Free-only/Ollama generation requires a fresh modal confirmation that previews the exact text and destination before `fetch`; strict JSON and a copied `groundingCue` are verified locally. The audio Blob is never sent, and the UI explicitly disclaims STT, recording understanding, pronunciation scoring, and fluency scoring. Evidence stores a bounded excerpt, full-source SHA-256, question, cue, provider/model/consent, and boundary; DWNB and merge preserve it, while immediate recording privacy deletion strips the source excerpt.
- P0 `browser-webgpu-model-v1` adds a real optional in-browser model path. Settings checks secure context, `navigator.gpu`, a real adapter, a 4 GB declared-memory floor, 128 MiB buffer limit, and browser quota; then requires an explicit 130–150 MB download opt-in. The pinned multilingual MiniLM q8 model runs `feature-extraction` in a dedicated Worker and ranks four authored follow-up candidates by cosine similarity. Runtime/model/license freshness blocks new installs when stale, partial cache is deleted on failure, and one control deletes weights plus runtime. The 118 MB weights are excluded from Git/ZIP/default Offline packs. The Transformers plaintext is Git-ignored and materialized by `npm ci`/prebuild from an XOR+gzip packed payload only after packed/output SHA-256 verification, avoiding the upstream minified Mistral-key push-protection false positive without bypassing GitHub. ONNX artifacts and Apache/MIT notices remain directly vendored; `npm audit` remains zero. No silent WASM/paid fallback, STT, acoustic score, or CEFR judgment is claimed.
- Listening practice enforces one-play rules and hides transcripts until submission. It prefers complete generated MP3/segment sequences when present and falls back to the browser's free German SpeechSynthesis; both paths are explicitly labeled non-exam-grade.
- Writing practice preserves provider-specific timing and task shape, stores drafts locally, and offers deterministic/self-review without fabricating an official score.
- Speaking practice preserves Goethe's two-topic four-minute presentation shape and telc's distinct experience-report and source-based discussion parts, with local MediaRecorder evidence and no fabricated partner interaction or acoustic score.
- Complete extended library route `/library` with 80 original readings and 80 listening scripts across A1–B2, level filtering, and 320 deterministic comprehension checks.
- Generated-audio batches 1–8 complete: 80/80 library listening items across A1–B2 have committed MP3 files (about 5.8 MB total), native playback, measured durations, byte sizes, SHA-256 checksums, one-speaker metadata, provenance, and Browser-TTS fallback. Library data now truthfully reports `generated-file-with-browser-tts-fallback` instead of the obsolete `browser-tts-only` source label.
- A resilient audio probe is used in onboarding and adaptive diagnostic: the native player stays hidden until positive finite metadata loads, displays the expected manifest duration, exposes Browser SpeechSynthesis at all times, shows retry/failure status instead of a permanent `0:00`, and never reveals the transcript. Runtime Service Worker v4 no longer stores HTTP Range/206 audio fragments. Bitstream tests parse MPEG Layer III headers, frame chains, duration, size, and payload variation for all 260 physical MP3 files and specifically require every diagnostic track to exceed ten seconds.
- Lesson-audio batches 1–9 complete: all 91/91 A1–B2 lessons have committed MP3 listening files (14.0 MB MP3 plus 10.9 MB optional Opus), a separate manifest with durations/sizes/SHA-256/provenance, native lesson playback, source disclosure, and Browser-TTS fallback.
- Exam-audio batches 1–10 complete: 96 generated MP3 files (about 8.2 MB) cover all 90/90 logical clips, all 7/7 targeted listening tasks, and all 35/35 full-simulation listening tasks. Manifest v2 discovers and audits the complete 42-task/90-clip listening inventory, reports targeted and full-simulation coverage separately, and confirms every task has every required logical clip with a complete ordered segment chain. Provider/task/clip ownership, duration, size, SHA-256, provenance, single-speaker status, and the non-exam boundary are verified. The listening runner prefers generated MP3, preserves Browser TTS as fallback, and plays ordered segments under one play limit. The Offline pack reads all three audio manifests, and the exam hub displays complete per-simulation MP3 coverage.
- Dedicated `/shadowing` studio over all 80 generated files with A1–B2 filtering, item selection, 0.75×/1×/1.15× playback, delayed transcript reveal, local MediaRecorder capture, model/attempt duration comparison, self-rating, reflection, and IndexedDB evidence. It explicitly avoids fabricated acoustic scoring.
- Local `/search` route with 3,512 unique contextual entries: all 96 lesson overviews, phrases, grammar blocks, reading glossaries, common errors, 160 library items, and 150 provider-scoped exam tasks. Ranking supports German umlaut/ß folding, Arabic diacritic/alef/definite-article normalization, source and CEFR filters, contextual return links, and no AI/network dependency.
- IndexedDB schema v3 with per-lesson progress, exercise attempts, daily sessions, error-clinic transfers, auditable initial/delayed review events, structured writing/speaking/mediation evidence, continuous exam sessions, and structured tutor interactions. Zod defaults preserve older schema-v3 snapshots, while v1/v2 migration, deterministic merge, and DWNB export/import include the new evidence.
- Versioned `.dwnb` v2/v3 export/import with per-file SHA-256, optional media, backward compatibility, archive-size/path guards, and optional PBKDF2/AES-GCM passphrase encryption.
- P0 atomic portability commit now writes the primary state, active profile registry, active-profile metadata, imported media, and pre-import restore point inside one IndexedDB transaction. New-profile import also preserves the displaced active profile in that same transaction. The UI adopts the already-committed state without issuing a second persistence write.
- Fault-injection tests abort after the primary write, after the restore point, and after media writes and prove rollback leaves the old state, media store, and restore slot untouched. Separate export interruption hooks prove no partial Blob is returned, while a physically truncated DWNB is rejected before mutation. `fake-indexeddb` is test-only.
- Dry-run import preview, deterministic Merge/Replace modes, automatic IndexedDB pre-import restore point, and one-click reversible atomic restore that swaps the displaced state back into the restore slot.
- Multi-profile IndexedDB registry with active-profile persistence, profile switching, and Import-as-new-profile with namespaced recording IDs.
- P0 immediate privacy controls independently remove all recording blobs linked to the active profile, all structured tutor history/evaluations, or the API key/Ollama endpoint from Session Storage. Recording deletion also removes stale draft-media links while preserving non-audio duration/self-review metadata; each destructive IndexedDB action has an explicit confirmation and reports the exact affected count.
- PWA shell/runtime cache with immediate service-worker activation, safe navigation-only fallback, and an optional user-controlled content pack. Offline manifest v2 keeps the complete 306-route registry and exposes independently selectable A1/A2/B1/B2/full packs with 58/58/58/210/309 routes; lower levels cannot leak another level or B2 exam routes. The worker extracts and stages versioned Next.js assets before atomically replacing the active pack.
- P0 selective Offline delivery now makes all generated audio explicitly opt-in instead of silently bundling it. Before download, Settings chooses Opus when supported or MP3 fallback and filters the selected payload to 40/48/48/124/260 files for A1/A2/B1/B2/full; after promotion, the worker measures and stores the actual cache byte size, route/asset/entry count, audio-entry count, and `includesAudio` state.
- Audio-only removal deletes every `/audio/` Cache Storage entry and rewrites pack metadata while retaining all cached lesson/exam documents, static assets, IndexedDB progress, personal recordings, and DWNB backups. Production-browser tests first install isolated A1 and prove A2/exam routes are absent, then install the audio-enabled full pack, prove an unvisited B2 lesson/exam/Shadowing flow offline, remove audio, and verify routes remain. `postbuild` now emits deterministic gzip bytes for built route HTML and unique Next assets before download, with the actual installed Cache Storage measurement retained afterward.
- `offline-size-manifest` is regenerated after every production build and records exact build-local raw/gzip totals, unique Next assets, and a SHA-256 fingerprint for all five packs. Values are not hardcoded in handoff docs because legitimate build-specific route output can change them; the UI reads the committed/generated manifest directly. The self-manifest response is excluded to avoid recursive sizing.

- ADR-075 adds the phoneme and free-correction engines without opening either claim. `german-phoneme-reference-v1` resolves a word only to human-authored IPA (the Arabic-learner inventory plus each lesson's own pronunciation items), keeps both authored readings when one spelling carries two stresses, and refuses to guess otherwise. `german-phoneme-alignment-v1` implements Needleman-Wunsch phoneme alignment with deletion/substitution/vowel-length classes, accepted r-variants, the three confirmed/needs-retry/technical-unverifiable states, and a same-attempt sentence rule with no composite score; `phoneme-observation-policy-v1` keeps all five readiness flags `false` behind a nine-step evidence ledger (no pinned revision, no ONNX/q8 artefact, no device measurement, no calibration, no native/learner trial, no independent phonetic review). `german-grammar-signals-v1` expands the local writing checker to nine deterministic rules with verbatim excerpts, German restatements, Arabic explanations, two certainty levels, and a visible unresolved channel. `writing-review-grounding-gate-v1` refuses Gemini notes that do not quote the sent text, rewrite it whole, claim an official score/grade/CEFR/pass result in a finding or the summary, mix exam brands, or hide open questions, and forces `needsHumanReview` when a high-confidence finding lacks a rule or a drill. `speaking-review-lanes-v1` plus `speaking-confirmed-transcript-v1` require the learner to confirm the local Whisper transcript before any send, re-ask when the text changes afterwards, and render acoustic, linguistic, and task-fulfilment as three separate lanes with `compositeScore: null`; audio is never part of a request. New persistence fields are optional, so v1 exports still validate. 56 new unit tests (978/978 across 143 files) cover the authored-reference rule, the alignment states, the closed gate, the nine grammar rules plus zero `proven` false positives over 84 authored model answers, every gate refusal, and the transcript-confirmation refusal paths.
